How to save HTML?

A quick and simple way to host your content online

ğŸ“ Simple steps to follow:
  • 01.Drag and drop file, paste code, or write your HTML/text in the editor. (Mandatory)
  • 02.Provide a custom name for a unique URL. (Optional)
  • 03.Set a password to edit your content later. (Optional)
  • 04.Click Submit to Save to publish your page.
  • 05.Get your rendered link instantly and share it!
Check out our Conditions for Hosting before starting.

How Does it Work?

We help you to save and host your HTML pages along with CSS, JavaScript, JSON and TXT files online for free. With our drag and drop upload, or paste your code on the editor field, you can easily host your projects in seconds. This service provides a simple playground for experimenting with HTML code and paste hosting.
